@Composable
fun NovaConnectControl(
    controlType: GlassConnectControl,
    vpnState: VpnState,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val haptic = LocalHapticFeedback.current
    val triggeredToggle = {
        haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.LongPress)
        onToggle()
    }

    when (controlType) {
        GlassConnectControl.ORB -> NovaOrbControl(vpnState, triggeredToggle, modifier)
        GlassConnectControl.ZED_GLASS -> NovaZedGlassControl(vpnState, triggeredToggle, modifier)
        GlassConnectControl.BUTTON -> NovaPillButtonControl(vpnState, triggeredToggle, modifier)
        GlassConnectControl.SWITCH -> NovaSwitchControl(vpnState, triggeredToggle, modifier)
        GlassConnectControl.SLIDER -> NovaSlideControl(vpnState, triggeredToggle, modifier)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun NovaOrbControl(
    vpnState: VpnState,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    val isConnected = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTED
    val isBusy = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTING || vpnState == VpnState.RECONNECTING
    val isError = vpnState == VpnState.ERROR
    val isPaused = vpnState == VpnState.PAUSED

    val ringColor by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = when {
            isConnected -> look.accent
            isBusy -> look.secondaryAccent
            isPaused -> look.warning
            isError -> look.danger
            else -> look.cardBorder
        },
        animationSpec = tween(420),
        label = "orb_color"
    )

    val pulse: Float
    val sweep: Float
    if (isConnected || isBusy) {
        val inf = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "orb_pulse")
        val animPulse by inf.animateFloat(
            initialValue = 1f,
            targetValue = 1.12f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(if (isBusy) 900 else 2200, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
            ),
            label = "pulse"
        )
        val animSweep by inf.animateFloat(
            initialValue = 0f,
            targetValue = 360f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(if (isBusy) 1500 else 5000, easing = LinearEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Restart
            ),
            label = "sweep"
        )
        pulse = animPulse
        sweep = animSweep
    } else {
        pulse = 1f
        sweep = 0f
    }

    val btnScale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isConnected) 1.03f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.65f, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMediumLow),
        label = "scale"
    )

    val source = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val desc = stringResource(if (isConnected) R.string.ctrl_disconnect_desc else R.string.ctrl_connect_desc)

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .size(216.dp)
            .semantics { contentDescription = desc }
            .testTag("connect_orb_button"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().scale(pulse)) {
            val r = size.minDimension / 2f
            drawCircle(
                brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                    colors = listOf(ringColor.copy(alpha = 0.30f), Color.Transparent),
                    center = center,
                    radius = r
                ),
                radius = r
            )
            if (isBusy || isConnected) {
                drawArc(
                    brush = Brush.sweepGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color.Transparent,
                            look.accent.copy(alpha = 0.90f),
                            look.secondaryAccent.copy(alpha = 0.90f),
                            Color.Transparent
                        )
                    ),
                    startAngle = sweep,
                    sweepAngle = 220f,
                    useCenter = false,
                    style = Stroke(width = 3.5.dp.toPx(), cap = StrokeCap.Round)
                )
            }
        }

        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(154.dp)
                .glassPress(source, scale = 0.93f)
                .scale(btnScale)
                .liquidGlass(shape = CircleShape, elevated = true)
                .clickable(
                    interactionSource = source,
                    indication = null,
                    role = Role.Button,
                    onClick = onToggle
                ),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = when {
                        isPaused -> GlassIcons.Pause
                        isConnected -> GlassIcons.Shield
                        else -> GlassIcons.Power
                    },
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = when {
                        isConnected -> look.accent
                        isBusy -> look.secondaryAccent
                        isPaused -> look.warning
                        isError -> look.danger
                        else -> look.textPrimary
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.size(46.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(
                        when {
                            isConnected -> R.string.ctrl_active
                            isBusy -> R.string.ctrl_linking
                            isPaused -> R.string.ctrl_paused
                            isError -> R.string.ctrl_retry
                            else -> R.string.ctrl_connect
                        }
                    ),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = look.textPrimary
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun NovaZedGlassControl(
    vpnState: VpnState,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    val isConnected = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTED
    val isBusy = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTING || vpnState == VpnState.RECONNECTING
    val isPaused = vpnState == VpnState.PAUSED
    val isError = vpnState == VpnState.ERROR
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(46.dp)

    val glowColor by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = when {
            isConnected -> look.accent
            isBusy -> look.secondaryAccent
            isPaused -> look.warning
            isError -> look.danger
            else -> Color.Transparent
        },
        animationSpec = tween(420),
        label = "zed_glow"
    )

    val shinePhase: Float
    val breathe: Float
    if (isConnected || isBusy) {
        val inf = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "zed_motion")
        val animShine by inf.animateFloat(
            initialValue = -0.35f,
            targetValue = 1.35f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(durationMillis = if (isBusy) 1300 else 3200, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Restart
            ),
            label = "zed_shine"
        )
        val animBreathe by inf.animateFloat(
            initialValue = 0.72f,
            targetValue = 1.0f,
            animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
                animation = tween(durationMillis = 1900, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
                repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
            ),
            label = "zed_breathe"
        )
        shinePhase = animShine
        breathe = animBreathe
    } else {
        shinePhase = -0.35f
        breathe = 0.85f
    }

    val source = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .size(216.dp)
            .glassPress(source, scale = 0.94f)
            .testTag("connect_zed_button"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        if (glowColor.alpha > 0f) {
            Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                val r = size.minDimension / 2f
                drawCircle(
                    brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                        0f to glowColor.copy(alpha = 0.48f * breathe),
                        0.55f to look.secondaryAccent.copy(alpha = 0.22f * breathe),
                        1f to Color.Transparent,
                        center = center,
                        radius = r
                    ),
                    radius = r
                )
            }
        }

        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(182.dp)
                .liquidGlass(
                    shape = shape,
                    elevated = true,
                    tintOverride = if (isConnected) look.accent.copy(alpha = 0.18f) else null
                )
                .clickable(
                    interactionSource = source,
                    indication = null,
                    role = Role.Button,
                    onClick = onToggle
                ),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            if (isConnected || isBusy) {
                Canvas(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .clip(shape)
                ) {
                    val cx = size.width * shinePhase
                    drawRect(
                        brush = Brush.linearGradient(
                            0f to Color.Transparent,
                            0.42f to Color.Transparent,
                            0.5f to Color.White.copy(alpha = if (isBusy) 0.34f else 0.16f),
                            0.58f to Color.Transparent,
                            1f to Color.Transparent,
                            start = Offset(cx - size.width * 0.4f, 0f),
                            end = Offset(cx + size.width * 0.4f, size.height)
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = when {
                        isPaused -> GlassIcons.Pause
                        isConnected -> GlassIcons.Shield
                        else -> GlassIcons.Sparkles
                    },
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.ctrl_toggle_vpn),
                    tint = when {
                        isConnected -> look.accent
                        isBusy -> look.secondaryAccent
                        isPaused -> look.warning
                        isError -> look.danger
                        else -> look.textPrimary
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(
                        when {
                            isConnected -> R.string.ctrl_protected
                            isBusy -> R.string.ctrl_linking
                            isPaused -> R.string.ctrl_paused
                            isError -> R.string.ctrl_retry
                            else -> R.string.ctrl_tap_shield
                        }
                    ),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = look.textPrimary
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun NovaPillButtonControl(
    vpnState: VpnState,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    val isConnected = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTED
    val source = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val bg = if (isConnected) look.accent.copy(alpha = 0.24f) else look.secondaryAccent.copy(alpha = 0.22f)
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(64.dp)
            .glassPress(source, scale = 0.97f)
            .liquidGlass(shape = RoundedCornerShape(32.dp), elevated = true, tintOverride = bg)
            .clickable(interactionSource = source, indication = null, role = Role.Button, onClick = onToggle)
            .testTag("connect_pill_button"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = GlassIcons.Bolt,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = if (isConnected) look.accent else look.textPrimary
            )
            Text(
                text = stringResource(if (isConnected) R.string.ctrl_disconnect_desc else R.string.ctrl_connect_desc),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = look.textPrimary
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun NovaSwitchControl(
    vpnState: VpnState,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    val isConnected = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTED || vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTING || vpnState == VpnState.RECONNECTING
    val source = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val thumbOffset by animateDpAsState(
        targetValue = if (isConnected) 74.dp else 8.dp,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.65f, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMediumLow),
        label = "switch_thumb"
    )
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .width(148.dp)
            .height(74.dp)
            .glassPress(source, scale = 0.95f)
            .liquidGlass(
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(37.dp),
                elevated = true,
                tintOverride = if (isConnected) look.accent.copy(alpha = 0.25f) else null
            )
            .clickable(interactionSource = source, indication = null, role = Role.Switch, onClick = onToggle)
            .testTag("connect_switch_button"),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .offset(x = thumbOffset)
                .size(58.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(if (isConnected) look.accent else look.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                .glassHighlight(CircleShape, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.55f), 0.8.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = GlassIcons.Power,
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.ctrl_toggle_vpn),
                tint = Color(0xFF041412)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun NovaSlideControl(
    vpnState: VpnState,
    onToggle: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    val isConnected = vpnState == VpnState.CONNECTED
    val density = LocalDensity.current
    var dragX by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(0f) }

    BoxWithConstraints(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(64.dp)
            .liquidGlass(shape = RoundedCornerShape(32.dp), elevated = true)
            .testTag("connect_slide_button")
    ) {
        val maxOffsetPx = with(density) { (maxWidth - 64.dp).toPx().coerceAtLeast(1f) }
        val baseOffset = if (isConnected) maxOffsetPx else 0f
        val currentOffset = (baseOffset + dragX).coerceIn(0f, maxOffsetPx)

        Text(
            text = stringResource(if (isConnected) R.string.slide_to_disconnect else R.string.slide_to_connect),
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
            color = look.textSecondary,
            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center)
        )

        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .offset { IntOffset(currentOffset.roundToInt(), 0) }
                .padding(4.dp)
                .size(56.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(if (isConnected) look.danger else look.accent)
                .glassHighlight(CircleShape, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.55f), 0.8.dp)
                .pointerInput(isConnected, maxOffsetPx) {
                    detectHorizontalDragGestures(
                        onDragEnd = {
                            val threshold = maxOffsetPx * 0.45f
                            if ((!isConnected && dragX > threshold) || (isConnected && dragX < -threshold)) {
                                onToggle()
                            }
                            dragX = 0f
                        },
                        onDragCancel = { dragX = 0f },
                        onHorizontalDrag = { _, delta -> dragX += delta }
                    )
                },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = GlassIcons.Power,
                contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.ctrl_slide_desc),
                tint = Color(0xFF041412)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun SpeedSparkline(
    samples: List<Pair<Long, Long>>,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    val downPath = remember { Path() }
    val upPath = remember { Path() }
    Canvas(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(48.dp)
    ) {
        if (samples.size < 2) return@Canvas
        val maxVal = samples.maxOf { maxOf(it.first, it.second) }.coerceAtLeast(1024L).toFloat()
        val stepX = size.width / (samples.size - 1).coerceAtLeast(1)

        downPath.reset()
        upPath.reset()
        samples.forEachIndexed { idx, (down, up) ->
            val x = idx * stepX
            val yDown = size.height - (down / maxVal) * (size.height * 0.85f) - 4f
            val yUp = size.height - (up / maxVal) * (size.height * 0.85f) - 4f
            if (idx == 0) {
                downPath.moveTo(x, yDown)
                upPath.moveTo(x, yUp)
            } else {
                downPath.lineTo(x, yDown)
                upPath.lineTo(x, yUp)
            }
        }
        drawPath(
            path = upPath,
            color = look.secondaryAccent.copy(alpha = 0.75f),
            style = Stroke(width = 2.dp.toPx(), cap = StrokeCap.Round)
        )
        drawPath(
            path = downPath,
            color = look.accent,
            style = Stroke(width = 2.5.dp.toPx(), cap = StrokeCap.Round)
        )
    }
}

// ===================== app/src/main/java/app/novavpn/ui/glass/GlassTheme.kt =====================
package app.novavpn.ui.glass

import androidx.compose.material3.ColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.CompositionLocalProvider
import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.compose.runtime.compositionLocalOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp
import app.novavpn.domain.model.GlassMaterial
import app.novavpn.domain.model.GlassPrefs
import app.novavpn.domain.model.GlassTint
import app.novavpn.domain.model.GlassWallpaper

@Immutable
data class GlassLook(
    val isDark: Boolean,
    val prefs: GlassPrefs,
    val dynamicColor: Boolean = false,
    val accent: Color,
    val secondaryAccent: Color,
    val textPrimary: Color,
    val textSecondary: Color,
    val textMuted: Color,
    val cardSurface: Color,
    val solidSurface: Color,
    val cardBorder: Color,
    val pillSurface: Color,
    val moduleSurface: Color,
    val chrome: Color,
    val chromeSolid: Color,
    val page: Color,
    val rimHi: Color,
    val rimMid: Color,
    val rimLo: Color,
    val highlight: Color,
    val platter: Color,
    val thumb: Color,
    val dim: Color,
    val sheet: Color,
    val separator: Color,
    val fill: Color,
    val fill2: Color,
    val shadow: Color,
    val danger: Color,
    val warning: Color,
    val success: Color,
    val latin: Boolean = true
) {
    val reduced: Boolean get() = prefs.reduceTransparency
    val translucent: Boolean get() = prefs.glassLists && !prefs.reduceTransparency

    fun pingColor(ms: Int?): Color = when {
        ms == null -> textMuted
        ms <= 0 -> danger
        ms < 200 -> success
        ms < 400 -> warning
        else -> danger
    }
}

val LocalGlassLook = compositionLocalOf {
    GlassLook(
        isDark = true,
        prefs = GlassPrefs(),
        dynamicColor = false,
        accent = Color(0xFF00D2E0),
        secondaryAccent = Color(0xFF6D7CFF),
        textPrimary = Color.White,
        textSecondary = Color(0x9EEBEBF5),
        textMuted = Color(0x4DEBEBF5),
        cardSurface = Color(0x612C2C30),
        solidSurface = Color(0xFF1C1C1E),
        cardBorder = Color(0x42FFFFFF),
        pillSurface = Color(0x661E1E20),
        moduleSurface = Color(0x612C2C30),
        chrome = Color(0x801C1D24),
        chromeSolid = Color(0xFF28282C),
        page = Color(0xFF000000),
        rimHi = Color(0x70FFFFFF),
        rimMid = Color(0x16FFFFFF),
        rimLo = Color(0x36FFFFFF),
        highlight = Color(0x30FFFFFF),
        platter = Color(0x1CFFFFFF),
        thumb = Color(0x38FFFFFF),
        dim = Color(0x78000000),
        sheet = Color(0xF21C1C1E),
        separator = Color(0x1FFFFFFF),
        fill = Color(0x5C787880),
        fill2 = Color(0x3D767680),
        shadow = Color(0x80000000),
        danger = Color(0xFFFF453A),
        warning = Color(0xFFFF9F0A),
        success = Color(0xFF30D158),
        latin = true
    )
}

fun resolveGlassTintColor(tint: GlassTint, isDark: Boolean = true): Pair<Color, Color> = when (tint) {
    GlassTint.TEAL -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF00D2E0) to Color(0xFF6D7CFF) else Color(0xFF00A7B5) to Color(0xFF6155F5)
    GlassTint.VIOLET -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF9E7BFF) to Color(0xFF00D2E0) else Color(0xFF7C4DFF) to Color(0xFF00A7B5)
    GlassTint.BLUE -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF0091FF) to Color(0xFF3CD3FE) else Color(0xFF007AFF) to Color(0xFF00C0E8)
    GlassTint.CYAN -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF3CD3FE) to Color(0xFF6D7CFF) else Color(0xFF0099CC) to Color(0xFF6155F5)
    GlassTint.MINT -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF00DAC3) to Color(0xFF3CD3FE) else Color(0xFF00A896) to Color(0xFF0088FF)
    GlassTint.INDIGO -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF6D7CFF) to Color(0xFFDB34F2) else Color(0xFF5856D6) to Color(0xFFAF52DE)
    GlassTint.PURPLE -> if (isDark) Color(0xFFDB34F2) to Color(0xFF9E7BFF) else Color(0xFFCB30E0) to Color(0xFF7C4DFF)
    GlassTint.PINK -> if (isDark) Color(0xFFFF375F) to Color(0xFF9E7BFF) else Color(0xFFFF2D55) to Color(0xFF7C4DFF)
    GlassTint.RED -> if (isDark) Color(0xFFFF453A) to Color(0xFFFF9F0A) else Color(0xFFFF383C) to Color(0xFFFF8D28)
    GlassTint.ORANGE -> if (isDark) Color(0xFFFF9F0A) to Color(0xFFFF375F) else Color(0xFFFF8D28) to Color(0xFFFF2D55)
    GlassTint.GREEN -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF30D158) to Color(0xFF00D2E0) else Color(0xFF28A745) to Color(0xFF00A7B5)
    GlassTint.BROWN -> if (isDark) Color(0xFFB78A66) to Color(0xFFFF9F0A) else Color(0xFFAC7F5E) to Color(0xFFFF8D28)
    GlassTint.GRAPHITE -> if (isDark) Color(0xFF98989D) to Color(0xFF636366) else Color(0xFF636366) to Color(0xFF8E8E93)
}

fun wallpaperStops(
    wallpaper: GlassWallpaper,
    isDark: Boolean,
    dynamicColor: Boolean = false,
    colorScheme: ColorScheme? = null
): List<Color> {
    if (dynamicColor && colorScheme != null) {
        if (wallpaper == GlassWallpaper.PLAIN) {
            return if (isDark) {
                listOf(lerp(colorScheme.background, Color.Black, 0.35f), colorScheme.background)
            } else {
                listOf(colorScheme.background, colorScheme.surface)
            }
        }
        return if (isDark) {
            listOf(
                lerp(colorScheme.background, colorScheme.primaryContainer, 0.28f),
                lerp(colorScheme.surface, colorScheme.tertiaryContainer, 0.18f),
                lerp(colorScheme.background, Color.Black, 0.42f)
            )
        } else {
            listOf(
                lerp(colorScheme.background, colorScheme.primaryContainer, 0.36f),
                lerp(colorScheme.surface, colorScheme.tertiaryContainer, 0.24f),
                lerp(colorScheme.background, colorScheme.secondaryContainer, 0.20f)
            )
        }
    }

    return if (isDark) {
        when (wallpaper) {
            GlassWallpaper.LAGOON -> listOf(Color(0xFF031327), Color(0xFF051C36), Color(0xFF010812))
            GlassWallpaper.MIST -> listOf(Color(0xFF141820), Color(0xFF0B0D13), Color(0xFF040508))
            GlassWallpaper.DUSK -> listOf(Color(0xFF1A0712), Color(0xFF1A0D2E), Color(0xFF0A0410))
            GlassWallpaper.GRAPHITE -> listOf(Color(0xFF121317), Color(0xFF0B0C10), Color(0xFF050506))
            GlassWallpaper.PLAIN -> listOf(Color(0xFF000000), Color(0xFF050608))
        }
    } else {
        when (wallpaper) {
            GlassWallpaper.LAGOON -> listOf(Color(0xFFD5E9FF), Color(0xFFE1F0FC), Color(0xFFE8F3FA))
            GlassWallpaper.MIST -> listOf(Color(0xFFDCE3EE), Color(0xFFE5EAF2), Color(0xFFECEFF5))
            GlassWallpaper.DUSK -> listOf(Color(0xFFFBE6D8), Color(0xFFF5E0F0), Color(0xFFEFE4F8))
            GlassWallpaper.GRAPHITE -> listOf(Color(0xFFD8DEE8), Color(0xFFE0E5ED), Color(0xFFE7EBF1))
            GlassWallpaper.PLAIN -> listOf(Color(0xFFECEEF4), Color(0xFFF2F3F8))
        }
    }
}

fun computeGlassLook(
    isDark: Boolean,
    glassPrefs: GlassPrefs,
    dynamicColor: Boolean = false,
    colorScheme: ColorScheme? = null,
    latin: Boolean = true
): GlassLook {
    val dynamicScheme = if (dynamicColor) colorScheme else null
    val useDynamic = dynamicScheme != null
    val (accent, secondary) = if (dynamicScheme != null) {
        val primary = dynamicScheme.primary
        val sec = when {
            dynamicScheme.tertiary != primary -> dynamicScheme.tertiary
            dynamicScheme.secondary != primary -> dynamicScheme.secondary
            else -> lerp(primary, Color.White, 0.25f)
        }
        primary to sec
    } else {
        resolveGlassTintColor(glassPrefs.tint, isDark)
    }

    val tinted = glassPrefs.material == GlassMaterial.TINTED
    val reduced = glassPrefs.reduceTransparency

    val solidCell = when {
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark -> dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerHigh
        dynamicScheme != null && !isDark -> dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerLowest
        isDark -> Color(0xFF1C1C1E)
        else -> Color(0xFFF9FAFC)
    }

    val moduleSurface = when {
        reduced -> solidCell
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark && tinted ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerHigh, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.14f).copy(alpha = 0.86f)
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainer, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.10f).copy(alpha = 0.64f)
        dynamicScheme != null && !isDark && tinted ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerLow, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.14f).copy(alpha = 0.84f)
        dynamicScheme != null ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerLowest, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.10f).copy(alpha = 0.68f)
        isDark && tinted -> Color(0xD4262830)
        isDark -> Color(0x66282A32)
        tinted -> Color(0xBFEEF3FA)
        else -> Color(0x78F4F7FC)
    }

    val chromeSolid = when {
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark -> dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerHighest
        dynamicScheme != null && !isDark -> dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerLow
        isDark -> Color(0xFF28282C)
        else -> Color(0xFFF3F5FA)
    }

    val pillSurface = when {
        reduced -> chromeSolid
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark && tinted ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerHighest, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.16f).copy(alpha = 0.90f)
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerHigh, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.12f).copy(alpha = 0.78f)
        dynamicScheme != null && !isDark && tinted ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainer, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.16f).copy(alpha = 0.88f)
        dynamicScheme != null ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerLow, dynamicScheme.primaryContainer, 0.12f).copy(alpha = 0.74f)
        isDark && tinted -> Color(0xDC22232A)
        isDark -> Color(0x821C1D24)
        tinted -> Color(0xCCEAF0F8)
        else -> Color(0x8AF4F7FC)
    }

    val cardSurface = if (glassPrefs.glassLists && !reduced) moduleSurface else solidCell
    val pageBg = wallpaperStops(glassPrefs.wallpaper, isDark, useDynamic, dynamicScheme).first()

    val sheetColor = when {
        dynamicScheme != null && isDark ->
            lerp(dynamicScheme.surfaceContainer, dynamicScheme.background, 0.25f).copy(alpha = 0.96f)
        dynamicScheme != null ->
            dynamicScheme.surfaceContainerLowest.copy(alpha = 0.96f)
        isDark -> Color(0xF21B1C20)
        else -> Color(0xF5F4F6FA)
    }

    val textPrimary = when {
        dynamicScheme != null -> dynamicScheme.onSurface
        isDark -> Color.White
        else -> Color(0xFF0A0A0C)
    }
    val textSecondary = when {
        dynamicScheme != null -> dynamicScheme.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.88f)
        isDark -> Color(0xB8EBEBF5)
        else -> Color(0xA63C3C43)
    }
    val textMuted = when {
        dynamicScheme != null -> dynamicScheme.onSurfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.54f)
        isDark -> Color(0x6BEBEBF5)
        else -> Color(0x6B3C3C43)
    }
    val separatorColor = when {
        dynamicScheme != null -> dynamicScheme.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = if (isDark) 0.45f else 0.55f)
        isDark -> Color(0x24FFFFFF)
        else -> Color(0x223C3C43)
    }

    return GlassLook(
        isDark = isDark,
        prefs = glassPrefs,
        dynamicColor = useDynamic,
        accent = accent,
        secondaryAccent = secondary,
        textPrimary = textPrimary,
        textSecondary = textSecondary,
        textMuted = textMuted,
        cardSurface = cardSurface,
        solidSurface = solidCell,
        cardBorder = if (isDark) Color(0x3DFFFFFF) else Color(0x80FFFFFF),
        pillSurface = pillSurface,
        moduleSurface = moduleSurface,
        chrome = pillSurface,
        chromeSolid = chromeSolid,
        page = pageBg,
        rimHi = if (isDark) Color(0x70FFFFFF) else Color(0xCCFFFFFF),
        rimMid = if (isDark) Color(0x16FFFFFF) else Color(0x47FFFFFF),
        rimLo = if (isDark) Color(0x36FFFFFF) else Color(0x99FFFFFF),
        highlight = if (isDark) Color(0x30FFFFFF) else Color(0x88FFFFFF),
        platter = if (isDark) Color(0x22FFFFFF) else Color(0x12000000),
        thumb = if (isDark) Color(0x38FFFFFF) else Color(0xE8FFFFFF),
        dim = if (isDark) Color(0x78000000) else Color(0x36000000),
        sheet = sheetColor,
        separator = separatorColor,
        fill = if (isDark) Color(0x5C787880) else Color(0x33787880),
        fill2 = if (isDark) Color(0x3D767680) else Color(0x1A767680),
        shadow = if (isDark) Color(0x70000000) else Color(0x1A000000),
        danger = if (isDark) Color(0xFFFF453A) else Color(0xFFFF383C),
        warning = if (isDark) Color(0xFFFF9F0A) else Color(0xFFFF8D28),
        success = if (isDark) Color(0xFF30D158) else Color(0xFF34C759),
        latin = latin
    )
}

@Composable
fun GlassThemeProvider(
    isDark: Boolean,
    glassPrefs: GlassPrefs,
    dynamicColor: Boolean = false,
    colorScheme: ColorScheme = MaterialTheme.colorScheme,
    latin: Boolean = true,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val look = remember(isDark, glassPrefs, dynamicColor, colorScheme, latin) {
        computeGlassLook(
            isDark = isDark,
            glassPrefs = glassPrefs,
            dynamicColor = dynamicColor,
            colorScheme = colorScheme,
            latin = latin
        )
    }

    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalGlassLook provides look, content = content)
}

// ===================== app/src/main/java/app/novavpn/ui/glass/GlassTilt.kt =====================
package app.novavpn.ui.glass

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorEvent
import android.hardware.SensorEventListener
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.DisposableEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.State
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.ui.geometry.Offset
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleEventObserver
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.LocalLifecycleOwner
import kotlin.math.abs

@Composable
fun rememberGlassTilt(enabled: Boolean): State<Offset> {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    val tiltState = remember { mutableStateOf(Offset.Zero) }

    DisposableEffect(enabled, context, lifecycleOwner) {
        if (!enabled) {
            tiltState.value = Offset.Zero
            return@DisposableEffect onDispose {}
        }
        val sensorManager = runCatching {
            context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as? SensorManager
        }.getOrNull()
        val gravity = runCatching {
            sensorManager?.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_GRAVITY)
                ?: sensorManager?.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER)
        }.getOrNull()

        if (sensorManager == null || gravity == null) {
            return@DisposableEffect onDispose {}
        }

        var lastCommittedX = 0f
        var lastCommittedY = 0f
        var isRegistered = false

        val listener = object : SensorEventListener {
            override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
                if (event.values.size < 2) return
                val nx = (event.values[0] / 9.81f).coerceIn(-1f, 1f)
                val ny = (event.values[1] / 9.81f).coerceIn(-1f, 1f)
                if (abs(nx - lastCommittedX) >= 0.025f || abs(ny - lastCommittedY) >= 0.025f) {
                    lastCommittedX = nx
                    lastCommittedY = ny
                    tiltState.value = Offset(nx, ny)
                }
            }

            override fun onAccuracyChanged(sensor: Sensor?, accuracy: Int) = Unit
        }

        fun register() {
            if (!isRegistered) {
                isRegistered = runCatching {
                    sensorManager.registerListener(listener, gravity, SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_UI)
                }.getOrDefault(false)
            }
        }

        fun unregister() {
            if (isRegistered) {
                runCatching { sensorManager.unregisterListener(listener) }
                isRegistered = false
            }
        }

        val observer = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
            when (event) {
                Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME -> register()
                Lifecycle.Event.ON_PAUSE, Lifecycle.Event.ON_STOP -> unregister()
                else -> Unit
            }
        }
        if (lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.RESUMED)) {
            register()
        }
        lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)

        onDispose {
            lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer)
            unregister()
        }
    }
    return tiltState
}

fun glassTiltAngleDegrees(tilt: Offset): Float =
    45f + (tilt.x * 35f - tilt.y * 20f).coerceIn(-55f, 55f)

private fun iosIcon(name: String, d: String, evenOdd: Boolean, mirror: Boolean): ImageVector =
    ImageVector.Builder(
        name = name,
        defaultWidth = 24.dp,
        defaultHeight = 24.dp,
        viewportWidth = 56f,
        viewportHeight = 56f,
        autoMirror = mirror
    ).addPath(
        pathData = PathParser().parsePathString(d).toNodes(),
        pathFillType = if (evenOdd) PathFillType.EvenOdd else PathFillType.NonZero,
        fill = SolidColor(Color.Black)
    ).build()

private fun buildTypography(isFa: Boolean): Typography {
    val bodyFamily = if (isFa) VazirmatnFamily else PlusJakartaFamily
    return Typography(
        displaySmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 32.sp, lineHeight = 38.sp),
        headlineMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 24.sp, lineHeight = 30.sp),
        headlineSmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 20.sp, lineHeight = 26.sp),
        titleLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 18.sp, lineHeight = 24.sp),
        titleMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 15.sp, lineHeight = 20.sp),
        bodyLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal, fontSize = 15.sp, lineHeight = 22.sp),
        bodyMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal, fontSize = 13.5.sp, lineHeight = 19.sp),
        bodySmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Normal, fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp),
        labelLarge = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, fontSize = 13.5.sp, lineHeight = 18.sp),
        labelMedium = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, fontSize = 12.sp, lineHeight = 16.sp),
        labelSmall = TextStyle(fontFamily = bodyFamily, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, fontSize = 11.sp, lineHeight = 14.sp)
    )
}

private class LocaleContextWrapper(
    base: Context,
    private val localized: Context
) : ContextWrapper(base) {
    override fun getResources(): Resources = localized.resources
}

@Composable
fun NovaVpnTheme(
    settings: AppSettings,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val isDark = when (settings.themeMode) {
        AppThemeMode.DARK -> true
        AppThemeMode.LIGHT -> false
        AppThemeMode.SYSTEM -> isSystemInDarkTheme()
    }
    val baseContext = LocalContext.current

    val colorScheme = when {
        settings.dynamicColor && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S -> {
            if (isDark) dynamicDarkColorScheme(baseContext) else dynamicLightColorScheme(baseContext)
        }
        isDark -> NovaDarkColorScheme
        else -> NovaLightColorScheme
    }

    val targetLocale = when (settings.language) {
        AppLanguage.FA -> Locale.forLanguageTag("fa")
        AppLanguage.EN -> Locale.ENGLISH
        AppLanguage.SYSTEM -> Locale.getDefault()
    }
    val isFa = targetLocale.language.startsWith("fa", ignoreCase = true)
    val direction = if (isFa) LayoutDirection.Rtl else LayoutDirection.Ltr

    val localizedContext = remember(baseContext, targetLocale) {
        val config = Configuration(baseContext.resources.configuration).apply {
            setLocale(targetLocale)
            setLayoutDirection(targetLocale)
        }
        LocaleContextWrapper(baseContext, baseContext.createConfigurationContext(config))
    }

    val dynamicActive = settings.dynamicColor && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S

    CompositionLocalProvider(
        LocalContext provides localizedContext,
        LocalConfiguration provides localizedContext.resources.configuration,
        LocalAppLanguage provides settings.language,
        LocalDigitMode provides settings.digitMode,
        LocalLayoutDirection provides direction
    ) {
        MaterialTheme(
            colorScheme = colorScheme,
            typography = buildTypography(isFa),
            shapes = NovaShapes
        ) {
            GlassThemeProvider(
                isDark = isDark,
                glassPrefs = settings.glass,
                dynamicColor = dynamicActive,
                colorScheme = colorScheme,
                latin = !isFa,
                content = content
            )
        }
    }
}

// ===================== app/src/main/java/app/novavpn/ui/theme/DigitLocale.kt =====================
package app.novavpn.ui.theme

import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.compositionLocalOf
import app.novavpn.domain.model.AppLanguage
import app.novavpn.domain.model.DigitMode
import java.util.Locale

val LocalAppLanguage = compositionLocalOf { AppLanguage.EN }
val LocalDigitMode = compositionLocalOf { DigitMode.LOCALE }

@Composable
fun isPersianUi(): Boolean {
    val lang = LocalAppLanguage.current
    return when (lang) {
        AppLanguage.FA -> true
        AppLanguage.EN -> false
        AppLanguage.SYSTEM -> Locale.getDefault().language.startsWith("fa", ignoreCase = true)
    }
}

@Composable
fun tr(en: String, fa: String): String {
    return if (isPersianUi()) fa else en
}

@Composable
fun fmtDigits(raw: String): String {
    val mode = LocalDigitMode.current
    val faUi = isPersianUi()
    val usePersian = when (mode) {
        DigitMode.PERSIAN -> true
        DigitMode.LATIN -> false
        DigitMode.LOCALE -> faUi
    }
    return if (usePersian) toPersianDigits(raw) else raw
}

fun toPersianDigits(input: String): String {
    val sb = StringBuilder(input.length)
    for (ch in input) {
        sb.append(
            when (ch) {
                '0' -> '۰'
                '1' -> '۱'
                '2' -> '۲'
                '3' -> '۳'
                '4' -> '۴'
                '5' -> '۵'
                '6' -> '۶'
                '7' -> '۷'
                '8' -> '۸'
                '9' -> '۹'
                else -> ch
            }
        )
    }
    return sb.toString()
}

fun formatDurationHms(totalSec: Long): String {
    val s = totalSec.coerceAtLeast(0L)
    val h = s / 3600L
    val m = (s % 3600L) / 60L
    val rem = s % 60L
    return if (h > 0) {
        String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d:%02d", h, m, rem)
    } else {
        String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d", m, rem)
    }
}

fun formatBytes(bytes: Long): String {
    val b = bytes.coerceAtLeast(0L)
    return when {
        b < 1024L -> "$b B"
        b < 1024L * 1024L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f KB", b / 1024.0)
        b < 1024L * 1024L * 1024L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f MB", b / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
        else -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f GB", b / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0))
    }
}

fun formatSpeedRate(bps: Long): String {
    val b = bps.coerceAtLeast(0L)
    return when {
        b < 1024L -> "$b B/s"
        b < 1024L * 1024L -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f KB/s", b / 1024.0)
        else -> String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f MB/s", b / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
    }
}

fun countryCodeToFlag(code: String?): String {
    if (code == null || code.length != 2) return "🌐"
    val upper = code.uppercase(Locale.US)
    val first = Character.codePointAt(upper, 0) - 0x41 + 0x1F1E6
    val second = Character.codePointAt(upper, 1) - 0x41 + 0x1F1E6
    return String(Character.toChars(first)) + String(Character.toChars(second))
}

package app.novavpn.ui.stats

import androidx.compose.animation.AnimatedContent

private enum class StatsSubTab { USAGE, DIAGNOSTICS }
private enum class UsagePeriod { DAILY, WEEKLY, MONTHLY }

@Composable
fun StatsScreen(
    dailyUsage: List<DataUsageDay>,
    sessions: List<SessionRecord>,
    diagnosticReport: DiagnosticReport,
    onRunDiagnostics: () -> Unit,
    onApplySafeFix: () -> Unit,
    onClearStats: () -> Unit,
    onTabBarShrinkChanged: (Boolean) -> Unit = {}
) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    var subTab by remember { mutableStateOf(StatsSubTab.USAGE) }
    var period by remember { mutableStateOf(UsagePeriod.WEEKLY) }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .statusBarsPadding()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .widthIn(max = 600.dp)
                .fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.stats_title),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium,
                color = look.textPrimary
            )
            if (subTab == StatsSubTab.USAGE && (dailyUsage.isNotEmpty() || sessions.isNotEmpty())) {
                GlassCircleButton(
                    icon = GlassIcons.Trash,
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.common_clear),
                    onClick = onClearStats,
                    size = 42.dp,
                    tint = look.textSecondary
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
        GlassSegmented(
            options = listOf(
                StatsSubTab.USAGE to stringResource(R.string.stats_tab_usage),
                StatsSubTab.DIAGNOSTICS to stringResource(R.string.stats_tab_diag)
            ),
            selected = subTab,
            onSelect = { subTab = it },
            modifier = Modifier
                .widthIn(max = 600.dp)
                .testTag("stats_subtab_selector")
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

        AnimatedContent(
            targetState = subTab,
            transitionSpec = { fadeIn(tween(220)) togetherWith fadeOut(tween(180)) },
            label = "stats_subtab_anim",
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
        ) { activeTab ->
            val listState = rememberLazyListState()
            ReportScrollShrink(listState = listState, onShrinkChanged = onTabBarShrinkChanged)
            LazyColumn(
                state = listState,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = 112.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                if (activeTab == StatsSubTab.USAGE) {
                    item {
                        GlassCard(modifier = Modifier.widthIn(max = 600.dp).fillMaxWidth()) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                                GlassSegmented(
                                    options = listOf(
                                        UsagePeriod.DAILY to stringResource(R.string.stats_daily),
                                        UsagePeriod.WEEKLY to stringResource(R.string.stats_weekly),
                                        UsagePeriod.MONTHLY to stringResource(R.string.stats_monthly)
                                    ),
                                    selected = period,
                                    onSelect = { period = it }
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                                val slice = when (period) {
                                    UsagePeriod.DAILY -> dailyUsage.takeLast(1)
                                    UsagePeriod.WEEKLY -> dailyUsage.takeLast(7)
                                    UsagePeriod.MONTHLY -> dailyUsage.takeLast(30)
                                }
                                val totalDown = slice.sumOf { it.downloadBytes }
                                val totalUp = slice.sumOf { it.uploadBytes }

                                Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween) {
                                    Column {
                                        Text(stringResource(R.string.stats_total_down), style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium, color = look.textSecondary)
                                        Text(
                                            text = fmtDigits(formatBytes(totalDown)),
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                                            color = look.accent
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End) {
                                        Text(stringResource(R.string.stats_total_up), style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium, color = look.textSecondary)
                                        Text(
                                            text = fmtDigits(formatBytes(totalUp)),
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                                            color = look.secondaryAccent
                                        )
                                    }
                                }

                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))
                                UsageBarChart(days = slice)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    item {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.stats_recent_sessions),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                            color = look.textPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.widthIn(max = 600.dp).fillMaxWidth().padding(top = 4.dp)
                        )
                    }

                    if (sessions.isEmpty()) {
                        item {
                            GlassCard(modifier = Modifier.widthIn(max = 600.dp).fillMaxWidth()) {
                                GlassRow(
                                    title = stringResource(R.string.stats_no_sessions),
                                    subtitle = stringResource(R.string.stats_no_sessions_sub),
                                    icon = GlassIcons.Timer
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        items(sessions, key = { it.id }) { session ->
                            val timeStr = remember(session.startedAtMs) {
                                DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("MMM d, HH:mm")
                                    .withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault())
                                    .format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(session.startedAtMs))
                            }
                            GlassCard(modifier = Modifier.widthIn(max = 600.dp).fillMaxWidth()) {
                                GlassRow(
                                    title = "${countryCodeToFlag(session.exitCountryCode)} ${session.profileName}",
                                    subtitle = fmtDigits("$timeStr · ${formatDurationHms(session.durationSec)} · ↓ ${formatBytes(session.downloadBytes)} / ↑ ${formatBytes(session.uploadBytes)}"),
                                    icon = GlassIcons.Timer
                                ) {
                                    GlassBadge(text = session.routeChain.name, color = look.accent)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    item {
                        GlassCard(modifier = Modifier.widthIn(max = 600.dp).fillMaxWidth()) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        Text(stringResource(R.string.stats_diag_title), style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium, color = look.textPrimary)
                                        Text(
                                            stringResource(R.string.stats_diag_sub),
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            color = look.textSecondary
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                    if (diagnosticReport.running) {
                                        CircularProgressIndicator(
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp),
                                            strokeWidth = 2.5.dp,
                                            color = look.accent
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        GlassCapsuleButton(
                                            text = stringResource(R.string.stats_run_test),
                                            icon = GlassIcons.Play,
                                            prominent = true,
                                            onClick = onRunDiagnostics,
                                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("run_diagnostics_button")
                                        )
                                    }
                                }

                                if (diagnosticReport.timestampMs > 0L) {
                                    GlassDivider()
                                    val exitLabel = if (diagnosticReport.exitIp != null) {
                                        "${countryCodeToFlag(diagnosticReport.exitCountry)} ${diagnosticReport.exitIp} ${diagnosticReport.exitCity?.let { "($it)" } ?: ""}"
                                    } else {
                                        stringResource(R.string.stats_unreachable)
                                    }
                                    GlassRow(
                                        title = stringResource(R.string.stats_exit_ip),
                                        subtitle = exitLabel,
                                        icon = GlassIcons.Globe
                                    )
                                    GlassDivider()
                                    DiagnosticStatusRow(
                                        title = stringResource(R.string.stats_dns_check),
                                        summary = diagnosticReport.dnsSummary,
                                        safe = diagnosticReport.dnsLeakSafe
                                    )
                                    GlassDivider()
                                    DiagnosticStatusRow(
                                        title = stringResource(R.string.stats_ipv6_check),
                                        summary = diagnosticReport.ipv6Summary,
                                        safe = diagnosticReport.ipv6LeakSafe
                                    )
                                    GlassDivider()
                                    Text(stringResource(R.string.stats_latency), style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge, color = look.textSecondary)
                                    val timeoutStr = stringResource(R.string.stats_timeout)
                                    diagnosticReport.latencies.forEach { ep ->
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                                        ) {
                                            Text("${ep.label} (${ep.host})", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = look.textPrimary)
                                            Text(
                                                text = if (ep.reachable && ep.latencyMs != null) fmtDigits("${ep.latencyMs} ms") else timeoutStr,
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFamily),
                                                color = if (ep.reachable) look.success else look.danger
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                    GlassDivider()
                                    Text(
                                        text = diagnosticReport.mtuSummary,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = look.textSecondary
                                    )

                                    GlassCapsuleButton(
                                        text = stringResource(R.string.stats_fix_conn),
                                        icon = GlassIcons.Wand,
                                        prominent = true,
                                        onClick = onApplySafeFix,
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("fix_connection_button")
                                    )
                                    diagnosticReport.safeFixAppliedNote?.let { note ->
                                        Text(note, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = look.success)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DiagnosticStatusRow(title: String, summary: String, safe: Boolean?) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    GlassRow(
        title = title,
        subtitle = summary,
        icon = if (safe == true) GlassIcons.CheckCircle else GlassIcons.Warning,
        iconTint = if (safe == true) look.success else look.warning
    )
}

@Composable
private fun UsageBarChart(days: List<DataUsageDay>) {
    val look = LocalGlassLook.current
    Canvas(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(120.dp)
    ) {
        if (days.isEmpty()) {
            drawLine(
                color = look.cardBorder.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                start = Offset(0f, size.height - 6f),
                end = Offset(size.width, size.height - 6f),
                strokeWidth = 2f
            )
            return@Canvas
        }
        val maxBytes = days.maxOf { it.downloadBytes + it.uploadBytes }.coerceAtLeast(1024L).toFloat()
        val count = days.size.coerceAtLeast(1)
        val slotWidth = size.width / count
        val barWidth = (slotWidth * 0.52f).coerceIn(8f, 36f)

        days.forEachIndexed { idx, day ->
            val total = (day.downloadBytes + day.uploadBytes).toFloat()
            val h = (total / maxBytes) * (size.height - 16f)
            val x = idx * slotWidth + (slotWidth - barWidth) / 2f
            val y = size.height - h
            drawRoundRect(
                brush = Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(look.accent, look.secondaryAccent)),
                topLeft = Offset(x, y),
                size = Size(barWidth, h.coerceAtLeast(6f)),
                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(6.dp.toPx(), 6.dp.toPx())
            )
        }
    }
}
